Theorem 218.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The bio variety V defined by convergence analysis constraints admits a stratification into 981 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 524 and intersection form of rank 4.

Proof: By the convergence analysis principle applied to bio theory, we decompose the problem into 981 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 524. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(4/n). QED.
